## Acceptance Criteria

**Conventions.** Format is GIVEN / WHEN / THEN. Ids are `AC-BDY-n`, kept stable from the draft, the QA review and every cross-reference in this GDD, so within a group they are **non-contiguous**; the groups A to N carry the order. A bare `D.n` is a Body formula; sibling formulas carry their owner's prefix (`PCI D.3`, `PIPE D.9`, `LY D.5`). Every number is the registry name's value or a Body formula's output, never a literal restated as Body's own. Tags: **(auto)** automated test; **(lint)** static check; **(review)** design-review of this document; **(manual)** human check with retained evidence; **(spike)** one-off engine spike with a measured number; **(perf)** a recurring performance test that fails when its threshold is exceeded. Placement: Logic criteria that need only a stubbed Layout live in `tests/unit/body/` (file `body_[feature]_test.gd`, functions `test_[scenario]_[expected]`); criteria that need the real Layout, Controller, Pipeline, Save/Load or Assembly registrations are marked *integration* and live in `tests/integration/body/`; recurring performance tests live in `tests/performance/body/`; shared fixtures in `tests/fixtures/body/`. Story-type classification for the evidence gate: Body is overwhelmingly **Logic** (BLOCKING, an automated test that passes) with an **Integration** slice (BLOCKING) and three **Visual/Feel** items, AC-BDY-73, AC-BDY-74b and AC-BDY-131 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: 131 added)* (BLOCKING, retained evidence in `production/qa/evidence/`, not waived at `qa.level: minimal`; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* AC-BDY-74 is split: 74a is an automated BLOCKING Logic/Integration gate, 74b the BLOCKING playtest, 74c a non-gating diagnostic); there is no Config/Data row, because Body's knobs are code constants.

**Harness rules that several criteria share.** (1) *Sink, not build lanes.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* Every criterion that exercises a mutator defect (AC-BDY-34, 40, 81, 83, 128 and the per-class criteria AC-BDY-132 to 135) runs ONCE, in the ordinary debug gdUnit4 runner, with an injected recording `plan_mismatch_sink` (BDY-21): the test asserts the returned `FAULT_CODE` (or `OK` for the saturate class), that nothing was written (the canonical bytes before and after are equal), and that the sink recorded `(mutator_id, code)`. There is no release-build test lane, because a GDScript `assert()` is compiled out of release and cannot be intercepted, so no mutator path uses one. The default sink (the counter `BDY_PLAN_MISMATCH`, active in every build, plus `push_error` in debug) is covered by one separate unit test that reads the counter. *Consistency* is defined once: `restore(snapshot())` accepts the block, actor ids are unique and below `next_actor_id`, and every `body_id` in either table is live in the Layout stub; it holds after EVERY call, including every call that returned a `FAULT_CODE`. (2) *One defect per restore fixture.* When several defects coexist in one save the first-reported reason is unspecified (it is not a contract); every restore fixture therefore carries exactly one defect. (3) *Fixtures with a Layout stub* take the rectangle set, offsets and return records from Layout's committed fixtures, cited by id and never by line number (`fixture_airlock` with `fixture_derelict_b_socket` for the airlock weld, `fixture_chain` for the chain detach, and `fixture_airlock`'s east wall plate with its mate's west plate for the hull-line plates) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)* Every BM4 call passes `cap_rects[]`; a fixture that does not name them passes the seam's hatch rectangles, which no fixture actor overlaps unless the criterion says so. (4) *Neighbour doubles* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was "Provisional neighbours"; every neighbour is Designed)* (Perception Filter, Sanity, Debug Overlay, Modular Ship Assembly, Web Map & Transit) are doubles where their code is not in the test build, and the criterion says so; it is revisited as each neighbour's story lands (AC-BDY-90). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: all of these GDDs are now on disk and Designed, so a criterion that was written against a stub is an INTEGRATION criterion against the real registration wherever the neighbour's code can exist in the test build; the criterion says "stub" only where the neighbour's implementation is not yet in `tests/integration/body/`'s reach, and then names the neighbour's own twin criterion. (5) **Hard CI proxies vs wall-clock** (K): the gdUnit4 CI runs a headless debug build on shared runners, so a wall-clock P99 can never be a CI gate; the hard gate is deterministic COUNTS, and the wall-clock P99 is a separate nightly benchmark. (6) **Clean-build scenarios** are the manifest `tests/fixtures/body/clean_build_manifest.yaml`, listing every scenario id by name (AC-BDY-62); "a clean build" in AC-BDY-29a, 29c and 62 means exactly those ids.)*

**Integration criteria: stub-backed or neighbour-owned *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.** An integration criterion is one of two kinds, so that no Body story waits on a story that does not exist yet. **Stub-backed** criteria run against the doubles of harness rule (4) at the Body story's close and count toward it, the real-registration twin being the neighbour's own story: AC-BDY-12, 29a, 36, 38, 41, 43 (its direct BM4 half), 44, 52, 53, 59, 65a, 65b, 65c, 65d, 87, 116, 119, 120, 121, 128, 130 and 135; AC-BDY-2 runs in this form with the registrations declared by the doubles using the documented `after` ids. **Neighbour-owned** criteria cannot pass until the neighbour's code exists in the test build, are owned and tracked BLOCKED-ON by that neighbour's story, and do NOT gate Body's story close: AC-BDY-2 in its real-registration form (Controller, Suit, Emitter, Graph), AC-BDY-29b (the real Suit), AC-BDY-42, the deferral half of AC-BDY-43 and AC-BDY-141 (Assembly), AC-BDY-74a (Assembly and Web Map & Transit), AC-BDY-84 and 123 (Perception Filter, Sanity), AC-BDY-92 and 92b (the scene harness and the Presenter) and AC-BDY-118 (the Pipeline). *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)* **Closure-gating integration criteria: AC-BDY-6 and 138** (the kernel's `LOADING` rebuild, KRN-BIND-01): they gate the epic, not the unit story, and run in the composition-root story.

### A. Registration, order and ownership

- **AC-BDY-1 (auto, unit) Block registration [BDY-5].** GIVEN boot, WHEN the registry is read, THEN `body.state` is registered with `block_id body.state`, class `SIM`, `persist true`, `persist_scope WORLD`, scope `SHARED`, `repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY`, `schema_version 1`, `supported_versions [1]`, `restore_after [layout.state]`, exactly one hook `body.integrate` and exactly one `PROJECTOR` subscriber `body.projector`.
- **AC-BDY-2 (auto, integration) Hook order [BDY-8].** GIVEN the boot topological sort over the REAL registrations (Controller, Layout, Suit, Emitter, Graph; no stubs), WHEN the order is computed, THEN `body.integrate` (divisor 1, offset 0, `after: [controller.step, layout.doors]`) runs after both, `emitter.place` runs after it, `suit.vitals` precedes `controller.step`, and a registration that names an unknown `after` id fails boot.
- **AC-BDY-3 (lint) Single writer of position [BDY-6, BDY-11].** GIVEN the Body module source, WHEN a static scan lists every assignment to `pos_curr`, `pos_prev`, `sub`, `last_octant` and `odometer`, THEN each lies inside `LocomotionStep` / `body.integrate` or inside BM1 to BM5 (an allow-list of function names kept in the test), and the only `controller.state` fields the module reads are `octant` and `v_eff` (never `loco_state`).
- **AC-BDY-4 (lint) Boundary with Layout [BDY-1].** GIVEN the Body module and this GDD, WHEN they are searched for a Body write to `next_body_id`, `welds[]` or `offset_px`, THEN there are zero writes (the three names appear in this GDD only as quoted Layout fields); Body allocates, retires and edits no `body_id`, membership, offset, rectangle, socket, seam or hatch.
- **AC-BDY-5 (auto, unit) Actor ids [BDY-20].** GIVEN `place_actor` called twice, `remove_actor` of the first, then `place_actor` again, WHEN ids are read, THEN they are 1, 2, 3 (never reused), the surviving rows hold ids 2 and 3, and `next_actor_id` (4) survives a snapshot and restore round trip.
- **AC-BDY-6 (auto, integration through the real load path; INTEGRATION CLOSURE GATE of Body's epic, run in the composition-root story) Seat binding is rebuilt after a load [BDY-7].** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the earlier form tested "a session-boot step" that no GDD defined.)* GIVEN the real kernel and Save/Load registrations, a world with two `SEAT` rows (`seat` 1 and `seat` 2, `actor_id` 5 and 9) and one `AGENT` row (`seat` 0), and an autosave of it, WHEN the kernel restores that autosave through `LOADING` (`request_restore`, every `restore()` then every `post_restore()`), the kernel's `LOADING` rebuild (CR-7, KRN-BIND-01) has run and one tick runs, THEN Body's pure read `seat_bindings()` returned `[(1, 5), (2, 9)]` in that order and nothing for the `AGENT` row, the kernel's binding table maps seat 1 to actor 5 and seat 2 to actor 9 with no datum other than the saved actors table, and a seat-1 command issued on that tick passes the authority stage (no `ACTOR_NOT_OWNED`). Body's epic does not close until this criterion and AC-BDY-138 pass. A unit-level half (auto, unit, not gated on the kernel) asserts `seat_bindings()` ascending order, `SEAT`-only content and that it adds no persisted field or byte to `snapshot()`.
- **AC-BDY-91 (auto, unit) `LocomotionStep` is pure [BDY-10].** GIVEN the script of `LocomotionStep`, WHEN inspected, THEN it extends `RefCounted` (not `Node`) and declares no member variables; a gdUnit4 test calls it with a `Callable` `collide` stub and no scene tree; WHEN called twice with identical arguments THEN the returned state, `moved` and `blocked` are identical and the input state object is unmodified. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the allocation-free hot-path form `step_into(state_idx, intent, collide, scratch)` (BDY-10), WHEN it runs the same 49 x 8 intent grid as the value-returning form on the same starts, THEN the committed row, `moved`, `blocked` and the exempt flag are identical to the value-returning form for every case.
- **AC-BDY-107 (auto, unit) Only seats are actors in the MVP [BDY-4].** GIVEN the MVP init fixture and a session driven by Interaction, Perception Filter and Horror stubs, WHEN 3,600 ticks run, THEN every `actors` row has `kind SEAT`, the row count equals the bound seats, and (lint) BM1 is called only from `init_world` and respawn.

### B. Locomotion (the 12 tests of PC-18 are Body's; PCI:202)

- **AC-BDY-7 (auto, unit) PC-18 test 1, cardinal walk [D.1].** GIVEN `v_eff` = `V_WALK` in a clear corridor, WHEN an actor walks in each cardinal octant (0 E, 2 N, 4 W, 6 S) for 60 ticks, THEN it moves exactly `V_WALK` px per tick (120 px in total) in that direction and `sub == (0, 0)` after every tick.
- **AC-BDY-8 (auto, unit) PC-18 test 2, diagonal cadence [D.1].** GIVEN a clear floor, octant 1 (NE), `v_eff` = `V_WALK` and `sub = (0, 0)`, WHEN 256 ticks run, THEN the per-axis displacement is **exactly** 362 px (x +362, y -362), `sub == (0, 0)` after tick 256, every tick's `moved` has equal magnitude on both axes (a 45-degree path), and ticks 1 to 8 give steps 1, 1, 2, 1, 2, 1, 1, 2 with `sub.x` 106, 212, 62, 168, 18, 124, 230, 80.
- **AC-BDY-9 (auto, unit) Scripted `v_eff` sequence [D.1].** GIVEN a scripted `v_eff` sequence 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3 on octant 0, WHEN stepped, THEN the actor moves 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3 px and `sub == (0, 0)` throughout. The run ramp itself (`RUN_RAMP_TICKS`, PC-18 test 3) is the Controller's and is asserted in the Controller's own test file; it is cross-referenced here and not duplicated.
- **AC-BDY-93 (auto, unit) West mirrors east [D.1].** GIVEN octant 3 (NW) at `v_eff` 1 from `sub = (0, 0)`, WHEN two ticks run, THEN tick 1 gives step x 0 and `sub.x == -181`, tick 2 gives step x -1 and `sub.x == -106`; the octant-1 (NE) twin gives step x 0 / `sub.x` 181 then step x +1 / `sub.x` 106; the x magnitudes are mirror images for all 256 ticks.
- **AC-BDY-94 (auto, unit) Diagonal run cadence [BDY-13].** GIVEN octant 1 at `v_eff` `V_RUN` (3) from `sub = (0, 0)`, WHEN 9 ticks run, THEN steps are 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 3 and `sub.x` runs 31, 62, 93, 124, 155, 186, 217, 248, 23; over 256 ticks the per-axis displacement is exactly 543 px and `sub == (0, 0)`.
- **AC-BDY-95 (auto, unit) Carry diagonal and its zero-motion ticks [D.1, BDY-9].** GIVEN octant 1 at `v_eff` `V_CARRY` (1), WHEN 256 ticks run on clear floor, THEN the first eight steps are 0, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 0, 1 (`sub.x` 181, 106, 31, 212, 137, 62, 243, 168), exactly 75 of the 256 ticks have `moved == (0, 0)`, total displacement is exactly 181 px per axis, and on every zero-motion tick `blocked` is false, `odometer` is unchanged and the `solid_in` stub is not called.
- **AC-BDY-16 (auto, unit) PC-18 test 11, carry [D.1].** GIVEN the carry gait on a cardinal octant, WHEN an actor moves, THEN it moves `V_CARRY` px per tick and a `run` flag changes nothing (Body sees only `v_eff`).
- **AC-BDY-10 (auto, unit) PC-18 test 4, stop [D.14, BDY-12].** GIVEN a diagonal move with `sub != (0, 0)`, WHEN the key is released (`v_eff` 0), THEN on that tick `sub == (0, 0)`, `last_octant == 8` (NONE), `moved == (0, 0)` and `odometer` is unchanged from the previous tick.
- **AC-BDY-17 (auto, unit) Reset predicate coverage [BDY-12, D.14].** GIVEN `v_eff` 0 with `octant` NE and `sub = (106, -106)` (STAGGERED), WHEN one tick runs, THEN `sub == (0, 0)` and `last_octant == NONE`; GIVEN an octant change from N to NE, THEN `sub` resets before the step; GIVEN a refused step, THEN `sub` is not reset; GIVEN a walk to run change inside one octant (PC-18 test 12), THEN `sub` is not reset.
- **AC-BDY-18 (auto, unit) Deceleration ladder, provisional [D.14, CP-8].** GIVEN `STOP_DECEL_TICKS` 3 in a test configuration, `octant` NONE, `last_octant` E and `v_eff` 2, 1, 0 on successive ticks, WHEN three ticks run, THEN the actor moves 2, 1, 0 px east and `sub` resets on the first `v_eff` 0 tick; at the default 0 the branch is never taken (the octant-9 and octant-NONE cases of AC-BDY-26); with `STOP_DECEL_TICKS` 3, `octant` NONE, `v_eff` 2 and `last_octant` NONE (8) the actor is IDLE and no `UNIT[8]` lookup is made *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **AC-BDY-85 (auto, unit) Tapping gives no speed [D.1, D.14].** GIVEN octant 1 at `v_eff` 2, WHEN a held run of 256 ticks is compared with a run that alternates one pressed tick and one released tick (128 pressed ticks), THEN the held run moves exactly 362 px per axis and the tapped run exactly 128 px per axis (each pressed tick starts from `sub = (0, 0)`, steps 1 px, and the release tick resets the residue).
- **AC-BDY-13 (auto, unit) PC-18 test 8, odometer [BDY-19].** GIVEN an actor walking into a wall, WHEN 60 ticks run, THEN `odometer` adds 0; GIVEN free walking, THEN `odometer += (abs(moved_x) + abs(moved_y)) * ODO_W[move octant]` post-collision (256 cardinal, 181 diagonal) and a synthetic value above 2^53 round-trips through the binary payload exactly.
- **AC-BDY-19 (auto, unit) Negative coordinates [BDY-35, D.1].** GIVEN an actor at `pos = (-40, -16)` and a rectangle set translated to negative coordinates, WHEN the PCI:908 and PCI:909 style runs repeat, THEN results equal the positive-coordinate runs; AND the engine-semantics fixtures `trunc_div(-181, 256) == 0`, `trunc_div(-362, 256) == -1`, `trunc_div(-768, 256) == -3`, `floor_div(-1, 48) == -1` hold when evaluated through the same expression the step uses *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `floor_div` is `trunc_div(a - posmod(a, b), b)`, BDY-35, so the fixtures add `floor_div(0, 48) == 0`, `floor_div(47, 48) == 0`, `floor_div(-47, 48) == -1`, `floor_div(-48, 48) == -1`, `floor_div(-49, 48) == -2`, and a property sweep of every `a` in -2,000..2,000 against `b` in {24, 48}: `floor_div(a, b) * b <= a < floor_div(a, b) * b + b`; `posmod` and the integer-division truncation were probed 2026-10-28 on the 4.7.2 editor binary, ADR-0010 P11; the release-export repeat is LINT-SPIKE-1)* (a failure is a spike finding, AC-BDY-72).
- **AC-BDY-26 (auto, unit) Invalid inputs [BDY-9].** GIVEN `octant` 9, `v_eff` -1 and `v_eff` 5, WHEN stepped, THEN `BDY_OCTANT_INVALID` counts and the octant is NONE; `v_eff` is clamped into `[0, 3]` for that tick only (-1 gives 0, 5 gives 3) and `BDY_VEFF_INVALID` counts; the hook does not fault. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN `octant` 9, `v_eff` 2 and `last_octant` 1 at the default `STOP_DECEL_TICKS` 0, THEN the actor is IDLE: `moved == (0, 0)`, `sub == (0, 0)`, `last_octant == 8` and `BDY_OCTANT_INVALID` +1; GIVEN `octant` NONE, `v_eff` 2 and `last_octant` 8, THEN the actor is IDLE, an instrumented `UNIT` accessor is never called with 8, `BDY_OCTANT_INVALID` +1 and the hook does not fault.
### C. Foot box, collision and sweep equivalence

- **AC-BDY-20 (auto, unit) Foot box [D.2, BDY-14].** GIVEN `pos = (304, 384)`, WHEN `foot_box` is evaluated, THEN it is `[296, 312) x [384, 392)` (the Layout close-safety fixture); GIVEN `pos = (100, 200)`, THEN `[92, 108) x [200, 208)`; the box has exactly `FOOT_BOX_W * FOOT_BOX_H` (128) cells.
- **AC-BDY-21 (auto, unit) First legal row [D.3].** GIVEN a wall footprint `[240, 264)` (PIPE:543) and an actor at rest on either side, WHEN it is walked flush, THEN south-flush `pos.y = y_foot = 264` and north-flush `pos.y = 232` (box `[232, 240)`); at rest `pos_prev == pos_curr`. Using a Presenter STUB that applies `floor(prev + (curr - prev) * tick_alpha)` (PIPE D.9) to Body's `pos_prev` and `pos_curr`, with `pos_prev.y = 130` and `pos_curr.y = 128`: `tick_alpha` 0.5 gives `p.y == 129` and `tick_alpha` 0.25 gives `floor(129.5) == 129` (both exactly representable). Body's obligation is only to supply `pos_prev` and `pos_curr` correctly; the interpolation is the Pipeline's.
- **AC-BDY-22 (auto, unit) No margin, one rectangle set [BDY-15].** GIVEN a `solid_in` stub that records its argument and returns a fixed set, WHEN a moving actor ticks, THEN the stub is called exactly once with the swept box of AC-BDY-75; a stationary actor makes zero calls; a rectangle the stub does NOT return is ignored by the sweep even when it lies in the actor's path; and at rest flush to a returned rectangle the box edge touches the rectangle edge with a gap of 0 cells (no margin).
- **AC-BDY-23 (auto, unit) Sweep equivalence [BDY-15, ADR-BODY-01].** GIVEN every start offset in a 48 x 48 window, every requested step `(step_x, step_y)` in -3..3 x -3..3 (49 pairs) and five fixture layouts (corridor, corner, doorway, 24-px wall, 1-px sliver), WHEN the production sweep and a reference stepper run (the reference is defined inline: 1-px sub-steps, x axis fully then y axis, refuse at the first overlapping pixel), THEN `moved` and `blocked` are identical for every case (2,304 starts x 49 steps x 5 fixtures = 564,480 enumerated cases, no random seeds). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Overlapping starts are covered too** (the exempt path is the most error-prone part of the sweep and the non-overlapping enumeration above cannot catch a divergence there): four more fixtures, each enumerated over every start in a 48 x 48 window whose foot box intersects at least one SOLID rectangle (so `E` is non-empty) and over the same 49 steps, using a **section-set stub** (a stub of `section_at` returning the fixture's sections, so exit containment is exercised without the real Layout): **F6** a single 24 x 96 rectangle; **F7** a box wholly inside one rectangle; **F8** the abutting pair `[672, 696)` and `[696, 720)` (the `fixture_airlock` east wall plate and its mate's west plate); **F9** the hatch cap of D.11 with plate B absent from the section set (the cap trap, exit containment refusing the sub-step to `x = 696`). The reference stepper for these fixtures is the same inline 1-px stepper extended with the BDY-16 predicate (per-rectangle overlap test plus exit containment). THEN `moved`, `blocked` and the final `E` (the set of rectangles intersecting the final box) are identical between the production clamp and the reference stepper for every case; the test prints the enumerated case count and fails on any divergence.
- **AC-BDY-24 (auto, unit) Narrow passage [BDY-15].** GIVEN two rectangles leaving a gap narrower than `FOOT_BOX_W` on the axis of travel, WHEN the actor presses through, THEN that axis is refused every tick, `blocked` is true when both axes refuse, and `|sub|` never exceeds 255.
- **AC-BDY-11 (auto, unit) PC-18 tests 5 and 6, sweep [BDY-15].** GIVEN the PCI:908 fixture (`pos = (100, 200)`, wall `[108, 132) x [150, 250)`), WHEN a NE walk runs, THEN `moved_x` is 0 on every tick, the box right edge never exceeds column 107 and the y axis keeps sliding; GIVEN the PCI:909 fixture (wall `[0, 400) x [100, 124)`, `pos = (200, 140)`, north at `v_eff` 3), THEN `pos.y` runs 137, 134, 131, 128, 125, 124 and holds, never entering the wall.
- **AC-BDY-96 (auto, unit) Slide residue [Edge Cases, D.1].** GIVEN the PCI:908 fixture and octant 1 at `v_eff` 2, WHEN 8 ticks run, THEN `moved.x` is 0 on every tick, `moved.y` is -1, -1, -2, -1, -2, -1, -1, -2, `sub.x` follows the free-run sequence 106, 212, 62, 168, 18, 124, 230, 80 (a refused step neither stores nor loses residue), `blocked` is false on every tick, and `odometer` after the 8 ticks is `11 * 181 = 1991`.
- **AC-BDY-97 (auto, unit) Flush cardinal and corner [Edge Cases].** GIVEN an actor flush against a wall to its east, WHEN octant 0 at `v_eff` 2 is held for 60 ticks, THEN `moved == (0, 0)`, `sub == (0, 0)` on every tick, `blocked` is true and `odometer` is unchanged; GIVEN a corner and octant 1, THEN both axes are refused, `blocked` is true and `|sub|` never exceeds 255.
- **AC-BDY-98 (auto, unit) Degenerate `solid_in` results [Edge Cases, BDY-32].** GIVEN a stub returning the empty set, THEN the full request is granted (`moved == step`); GIVEN a stub returning the same rectangle twice, and the same set in reverse key order, THEN `moved` and `blocked` equal the ordered, unique result, and in a debug build the debug signal `diag.solid_in_unordered` fires.
- **AC-BDY-75 (auto, unit) Swept box and test count [D.12, BDY-15].** GIVEN `pos = (200, 140)` and `step = (0, -3)`, WHEN the swept box is built, THEN it is `[192, 208) x [137, 148)` (16 by 11 cells); GIVEN every `abs(step_x)` and `abs(step_y)` in 0 to 3, THEN width is `16 + abs(step_x)`, height is `8 + abs(step_y)`, exactly one `solid_in` call is issued, and with `BROADPHASE_CELL_PX` 48 (Layout, LY:228) the box touches at most 4 broadphase cells (at most 2 per axis for any cell size of at least `FOOT_BOX_W + 3` = 19 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the bound is the coupling `BROADPHASE_CELL_PX >= FOOT_BOX_W + 3`, so the test also runs with `BROADPHASE_CELL_PX` = 19 and asserts the bound holds, and with `FOOT_BOX_W` 24 and a 24-px cell, a deliberate out-of-range configuration, asserting the bound FAILS, so the stated range is the boundary)*); GIVEN a fixture whose `solid_in` returns `K = 16` rectangles, WHEN the literal 1-px loop is instrumented, THEN the box-versus-rectangle tests are at most `7 * 16 = 112`.
- **AC-BDY-12 (auto, integration) PC-18 test 7, door leaf in the same tick [BDY-8, BDY-18].** GIVEN a leaf rectangle that `layout.doors` closes in P3 of tick T, WHEN the actor presses into the doorway, THEN it is stopped at the leaf in tick T itself; a leaf that OPENS in tick T lets the actor pass in tick T; the test uses the real hook order (a reordered registration would pass a tick late, which AC-BDY-2 also catches).
- **AC-BDY-25 (auto, unit) Actors do not block one another [BDY-17].** GIVEN two actors, WHEN one walks onto the other's pixel, THEN both positions are legal, `moved` is unchanged and no diagnostic fires; the door-close rejection still sees every actor's foot box (`foot_boxes(body_id)` lists all, ascending).
- **AC-BDY-86 (auto, unit) No body-blocking [BDY-17].** GIVEN actor B stationary in a one-tile corridor and actor A walking through B's pixels, WHEN 60 ticks run, THEN A's `pos_curr` sequence equals the sequence of the same run with B absent, and neither actor's `moved` changes.

### D. Overlap exemption (the body is never displaced)

- **AC-BDY-27 (auto, unit) Per-rectangle predicate [BDY-16, D.11].** GIVEN a box at `pos = (690, 282)` astride the plate `[672, 696) x [240, 336)`, WHEN each 1-px sub-step is tested, THEN west (`ov` 120 > 112) is refused, east (104) and north or south (112) are admitted, and `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` increments on every exempt tick on which the actor requested a step (BDY-16 counting rule; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the same actor STATIONARY for 60 ticks after a transfer placed it there, THEN the counter increments exactly once, at the detection event, adds nothing on the 60 idle ticks, and resumes incrementing on each tick the actor presses a key — option (a); *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* the exact deltas are +1 at the event, 0 on each of the 60 idle ticks, +1 on each following tick with a non-zero requested step, and 0 on a carry-diagonal zero-step tick, which is `STATIONARY`).
- **AC-BDY-28 (auto, unit) Abutting rectangles [BDY-16, D.11].** GIVEN a box at `pos.x = 696` astride abutting exempt rectangles `[672, 696)` and `[696, 720)`, WHEN east is tested, THEN it is refused (the east rectangle's `ov` grows from 64 to 72) although the total stays 128; moves along the shared edge and away are admitted.
- **AC-BDY-29 (auto, unit) Exit containment: the only exits lead back onto the body's floor [BDY-16, D.11].** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)* GIVEN a body whose Layout state holds plate A `[672, 696) x [240, 336)` SOLID and NOT plate B (B belongs to a section that is not part of this body), and a snapshot authored with an actor at `pos = (690, 282)` restored against that Layout state, WHEN it steps east at `V_WALK` for 8 ticks, THEN `E = {A}`, no sub-step increases `ov(box, A)`, `pos.x` runs 690, 692, 694, 695 and stays at 695 (the sub-step to 696 is refused because its destination origin lies in no section of the body), `actor_section` answers section 1 on every tick, `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` increments on every tick, and `pos` is unchanged on every tick the actor does not step (no push). GIVEN a second fixture in which the destination origin of the same east step lies in a section of the body (an open doorway beyond a closed leaf), THEN the step is admitted. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **The full admitted-move set is asserted for each direction, not the east run alone** (fixture assumption stated in D.11: the hull-wall rectangles continue north and south of the plate and are not in `E` at the start): at `pos = (690, 282)` east is admitted for sub-steps to `x` 691 to 695 and refused at 696; west is refused at once; north is admitted down to `y = 240` and refused at 239; south is admitted down to `y = 328` and refused at 329; a held NE input converges to `(695, 240)` and a held SE input to `(695, 328)`; and the strip start `pos = (689, 282)` has west refused (`ov` 120 to 128) while `pos = (688, 282)` has west admitted (`ov` 128 to 128) and the actor walks back onto the floor.
- **AC-BDY-29b (auto, integration: Body + Layout + Suit) The cap-trap actor reads EXPOSED [BDY-16, BDY-29, D.11].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* GIVEN the D.11 fixture (a snapshot authored with the actor at `(695, 282)` restored against the Layout state holding plate A SOLID) with the real Layout and the real Suit registration *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*, and the actor at the resting pose `pos = (695, 282)`, WHEN `actor_section` is read, THEN it answers section 1 and `actor_local_px = (695, 282)`; WHEN Layout's `room_at(1, (695, 282))` is read, THEN it answers NONE (inside plate A's footprint); WHEN `suit.vitals` runs, THEN Suit is in its EXPOSED state for that seat by its own rule (SE:342: `room_at` NONE and not `IN_POD`) with the exterior's values by rule (vacuum band, anoxic band, exterior temperature, no `draw_air`). GIVEN instead a closed door leaf under an exempt actor (a leaf closed by the real door hook), THEN `room_at` answers the door's `sill_room`, Suit is not EXPOSED, and the exposure rule does not apply. The result is the evidence that closes the Suit & Equipment row of 8.3 for this case, and the test fails if any sentence of the Body module or this GDD's lint list claims a trapped actor's readings are those of a room.
- **AC-BDY-29c (auto, unit + integration) Trapped-actor probe and the restored-while-trapped case [BDY-16, BDY-31].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* GIVEN the D.11 cap-trap fixture and an exempt actor at `pos = (695, 282)`, WHEN a snapshot authored with the actor at `(695, 282)` is restored against the Layout state holding plate A SOLID (the detection event is `post_restore` only; a cut can no longer create this state, BDY-24 (iii)) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*, or a BM5 transfer places it there, THEN the detection event (`post_restore` or the transfer) runs the bounded probe, `BDY_TRAPPED_ACTOR` increments exactly once for that actor, `trapped_actors()` lists it, `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` rose by exactly 1 at that event and by 0 on each following idle tick, `pos_curr` is unchanged on every following idle tick (no displacement, no rescue), the restore reports success, and a second restore of the same save reproduces the same single increment *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the exact deltas, and the list's recompute-on-read rule is AC-BDY-136)*; GIVEN `pos = (680, 282)` (box wholly inside the plate, west admitted), THEN the probe finds an exit and does not flag; GIVEN every scenario of the clean-build manifest (AC-BDY-62), THEN `BDY_TRAPPED_ACTOR` and `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` read 0 at the end of each.
- **AC-BDY-29a (auto, integration) A NONE section never occurs in a clean build [BDY-16, BDY-29].** GIVEN every scenario named in the clean-build manifest `tests/fixtures/body/clean_build_manifest.yaml` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the earlier "every integration scenario in sections C to H and J" had no defined set; the manifest lists the ids and nothing outside it is a clean-build scenario)* — at minimum `weld_absorbed_avatar`, `weld_host_avatar`, `detach_keep_side`, `detach_new_side`, `pod_hop_out_and_back`, `restore_mid_move_three_phases`, `door_close_open_same_tick`, `system_cut_deferred_until_clear`, `clean_route_10k` and `death_restart_from_autosave`, each a SYSTEM-origin cut only where Assembly's deferral releases it — WHEN each tick ends, THEN `actor_section` answers a section id for every actor on a Layout body (zero NONE answers); the deliberate-defect fixtures (an origin placed in an exterior gap) assert the opposite: NONE, `BDY_TRANSFER_NO_SECTION` incremented, and no exempt sub-step from any pose ends at a NONE origin.
- **AC-BDY-30 (review, backed by grep script G4) Residuals are named, not asserted [BDY-16].** GIVEN BDY-16, D.11 and Edge Cases, WHEN they are read, THEN the three residuals (a box wholly inside one 24-px rectangle; a box astride two exempt rectangles with neither side reaching open floor; the cap trap) are named and labelled defect-only, no sentence claims any of them is a safety property, no sentence says an exempt actor can leave the hull, and `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` in the debug overlay is named as the visibility mechanism. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the "no sentence claims ..." half is a mechanical check, not a reading.)* The check is the grep script G4 of 8.4: zero matches for `stuck, not (a )?safe` used as reassurance, `reading of a section it is still in`, `every air, heat and pressure reading`, `stuck, not unsafe` and `can leave the hull` outside quoted-struck text, and at least one match each for `visibility, not safety` in BDY-16, BDY-29 and D.11 and for `BDY_TRAPPED_ACTOR` in BDY-16, 3.7 and Acceptance.
- **AC-BDY-30a (auto, unit) Residual (i) is deterministic [BDY-16].** GIVEN a box wholly inside one 24-px rectangle, WHEN the same input stream runs twice, THEN the outcomes are bit-identical (determinism only; the exit side is NOT asserted).
- **AC-BDY-31 (auto, unit) Rectangles outside `E` stay solid [BDY-16].** GIVEN an exempt actor and a second solid wall not intersecting its box, WHEN it steps toward the wall, THEN the wall blocks it.
- **AC-BDY-32 (auto, unit) Exemption is not persisted [BDY-16, BDY-31].** GIVEN a snapshot taken while an actor is exempt, WHEN restored and ticked, THEN `E` is recomputed from the box, the exit continues identically, and the canonical bytes contain no exemption field.
- **AC-BDY-33 (auto + lint) No push-out exists [BDY-16].** GIVEN a transfer or restore that lands a box inside a SOLID rectangle, WHEN ticks run with no input, THEN `pos_curr` is unchanged on every tick (auto); AND the lint finds zero identifiers matching `push_out|pushout|shove|displace|nudge|unstick|depenetrat|snap_to_free` in the Body module (`push_back`, `push_front`, `push_error` and `push_warning` are allowed).
- **AC-BDY-130 (auto, unit + integration) Clean transfers and restores leave the counters at 0 [BDY-16, 3.2, 3.7].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN the clean-build manifest scenarios `weld_absorbed_avatar`, `weld_host_avatar`, `detach_keep_side`, `detach_new_side` and `restore_mid_move_three_phases`, none of which lands a foot box in a SOLID rectangle, WHEN each event completes, THEN `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` and `BDY_TRAPPED_ACTOR` are 0 immediately after the event and after 60 further ticks (a detection event on a clean box adds nothing). GIVEN an actor in the `STATIONARY` step-state (overlapping box, octant 1 at `V_CARRY`, a zero-step tick), THEN no `solid_in` call is made, `E` is not evaluated and the counter is unchanged on that tick.
- **AC-BDY-136 (auto, unit) `trapped_actors()` is never stale [BDY-16].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN an actor at the D.11 cap-trap pose `(695, 282)` that `trapped_actors()` lists, WHEN a mutator moves it to open floor (BM5 to a clear cell) or the Layout stub removes plate A from the SOLID set, THEN the very next `trapped_actors()` read no longer lists it, with no tick and no detection event in between; GIVEN the opposite (an actor outside the trap that a mutator places into it), THEN the next read lists it; the read writes nothing and the canonical bytes are identical before and after.
- **AC-BDY-104 (auto, unit) Overlap detection events [BDY-16].** GIVEN a weld, a pod hop and a detach with a Layout-defect fixture (the overlap is not the seam's cap, which BM4 refuses, AC-BDY-43) that each land a foot box inside a SOLID rectangle, and a restore of a save whose box overlaps a rectangle made SOLID since, WHEN each completes, THEN `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` increments by exactly 1 at that event (the event's box test finds `E` non-empty) and by 0 on each following idle tick, the debug `diag.actor_in_footprint` fires at the event, `pos_curr` is unchanged, and the restore reports success *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the "at that event or on the first stepping tick" disjunction is struck)*.

### E. Mutators and transfers

- **AC-BDY-34 (auto, unit; recording sink) Every mutator returns a status, `can_*` are pure [BDY-21].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN each of BM1 to BM7 called with arguments its `can_*` read rejects (one fixture per reason code of 3.5, except the BM4 range-overflow reasons, which are the saturate class of AC-BDY-133), WHEN called, THEN it returns the negated reason code as its `FAULT_CODE` (BM1 returns a negative value, never an id), the canonical bytes, `next_actor_id`, the dirty set and the event queue are identical before and after (check-then-write), the injected `plan_mismatch_sink` records `(mutator_id, code)` exactly once, and `restore(snapshot())` accepts the block; GIVEN each `can_*` read, THEN it opens no `WriteScope` and the canonical bytes are identical before and after.
- **AC-BDY-35 (auto, unit) Reason codes [BDY-20, BDY-21, BDY-26].** GIVEN each rejecting input, THEN `can_place` answers `BDY_PLACE_UNKNOWN_BODY`, `BDY_PLACE_INSIDE_FOOTPRINT`, `BDY_PLACE_CAPACITY`, `BDY_PLACE_BAD_KIND`, `BDY_PLACE_DUPLICATE_SEAT`; `can_remove` and `can_transfer` answer `BDY_UNKNOWN_ACTOR` for an unknown actor; `can_rebase_attach(surviving_body_id, absorbed_body_id, frame_shift_px)` answers `BDY_REBASE_SAME_BODY`, `BDY_REBASE_UNKNOWN_BODY` and `BDY_REBASE_SHIFT_OFF_GRID` (and BM4, at apply time only, `BDY_REBASE_SECTION_NOT_OWNED`, AC-BDY-40, and `BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP`, AC-BDY-140) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; `can_rebase_detach(old_body_id, frame_shift_px)` answers `BDY_REBASE_UNKNOWN_BODY`, and *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* `BDY_REBASE_POSE_OUT_OF_RANGE` and `BDY_VEL_OUT_OF_RANGE` (AC-BDY-128); `can_set_velocity` answers `BDY_VEL_UNKNOWN_BODY` and `BDY_VEL_OUT_OF_RANGE`.
- **AC-BDY-108 (auto, unit) Placed row [BDY-20].** GIVEN BM1 into a clear cell, THEN the row has `pos_prev == pos_curr`, `sub (0, 0)`, `last_octant 8`, `odometer 0` and the given `body_id`, `kind` and `seat`.
- **AC-BDY-111 (auto, unit) AGENT and seat rules on placement [BDY-7, BDY-20].** GIVEN `can_place` for two `AGENT` rows with `seat` 0, THEN both are accepted; for two `SEAT` rows with `seat` 1, THEN the second answers `BDY_PLACE_DUPLICATE_SEAT`; for a `SEAT` row with `seat` 0 or a negative seat, THEN the answer is `BDY_PLACE_BAD_KIND`.
- **AC-BDY-83 (auto, unit; recording sink) Over-cap placement [BDY-20, BDY-21].** GIVEN `actor_count == MAX_ACTORS`, WHEN `can_place` is read, THEN `BDY_PLACE_CAPACITY`; GIVEN a caller that ignores it, WHEN BM1 applies, THEN BM1 returns `-BDY_PLACE_CAPACITY`, writes no row, leaves `next_actor_id` unchanged and the sink records the code (no over-cap row exists, so there is no exception to consistency and `restore(snapshot())` accepts the block) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; GIVEN a save with `MAX_ACTORS + 1` rows, THEN restore rejects the whole block with `BDY_RESTORE_CAPACITY`.
- **AC-BDY-36 (auto, integration) Weld fixture [D.4, BDY-22].** GIVEN the airlock fixture `frame_shift_px = (576, 96)`, an absorbed body whose pose row EXISTS before the weld (`vel (3, 0)`) and an actor on it at `(100, 50)`, WHEN BM3 applies, THEN `pos_curr == (676, 146)`, `pos_prev == pos_curr`, `sub` and `last_octant` unchanged, `actor_section` and `actor_local_px` identical to before, `BODY_TRANSFER{weld}` raised for it only, a host actor at `(300, 100)` untouched, the absorbed pose row deleted (observable because it existed) and `BODY_DOCK_CONTACT` raised once.
- **AC-BDY-37 (auto, unit) Weld dirty keys [D.13].** GIVEN BM3 with 8 moved actors, WHEN it applies, THEN the marked set is 9 keys (8 `ACT` plus the deleted `BODY` row), each `(KIND << 32) | id` with `KIND` in {2, 4}, well under `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS`; `ACT/3` encodes to 17,179,869,187 and `BODY/1` to 8,589,934,593.
- **AC-BDY-99 (auto, unit) Dirty keys, hook and mutators [D.13].** GIVEN 8 actors (6 moving, 1 stopped with `sub` unchanged, 1 stopped with `sub` reset) and 8 bodies (3 with `vel != 0`), WHEN one tick runs, THEN the marked set holds one `ACT` key per actor whose `pos_curr` or `sub` changed and one `BODY` key per body with non-zero `vel`, at most 16 keys, no `ALL` report; BM1, BM2 and BM5 each mark exactly `ACT/<id>`, BM6 and BM7 each exactly `BODY/<id>`; the keys are marked even though the underlying write is a packed-array element write (4.7 no longer calls the setter, verified-from-docs).
- **AC-BDY-38 (auto, integration) Detach fixture [D.5, BDY-23].** GIVEN Layout's `fixture_chain` (sections 1 at `(0, 0)`, 3 at `(576, 96)`, 4 at `(1056, 96)`; cut seam (1, 3); `frame_shift_px (576, 96)`; new offsets 3 `(0, 0)` and 4 `(480, 0)`), `world_old = (1000, 500)`, and actors at `(300, 100)` (section 1), `(600, 200)` (section 3) and `(1100, 150)` (section 4), WHEN BM4 applies after Layout's mutator, THEN the section-3 and section-4 actors move by `-(576, 96)` to `(24, 104)` and `(524, 54)` onto `new_body_id` with `sub == (0, 0)`, `last_octant` kept and `pos_prev == pos_curr`; the section-1 actor is untouched; `actor_local_px` is unchanged for all three; `abs` is conserved (`(1600, 700)` on both sides for the section-3 actor); the new pose row is `(1576, 596)` with `vel_old`; and `BODY_TRANSFER{detach}` and `BODY_HULL_SEPARATION` are raised.
- **AC-BDY-39 (auto, unit) Detach side rule [BDY-23, PCI:651].** GIVEN an actor whose foot origin is `(690, 282)` inside section 1's east wall footprint and whose box reaches column 697, WHEN BM4 applies with `cap_rects[]` empty (the side rule in isolation; with the seam's real hatch plates the guard refuses first, AC-BDY-43) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*, THEN it stays on `C_keep` (the box is not split); GIVEN an origin in an exterior gap, THEN it stays on `C_keep`, `BDY_TRANSFER_NO_SECTION` increments and `actor_section` answers NONE.
- **AC-BDY-80 (auto, unit) Foot origin decides the side by its top row [BDY-14, BDY-23].** GIVEN an east-west seam line at row `Y` with section N above and section S below, and an actor at `pos.y = Y - 1` (box rows `[Y - 1, Y + 7)`, seven of eight rows in S), WHEN BM4 applies with `cap_rects[]` empty (the side rule in isolation) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*, THEN the actor is assigned by its origin row, which lies in N, and the box is not split; GIVEN `pos.y = Y`, THEN it is assigned to S.
- **AC-BDY-40 (auto, unit; recording sink) Call-order trap [BDY-23].** GIVEN BM4 invoked after Layout's mutator, THEN the pre-cut offsets are reconstructed as `section_offset + frame_shift_px` for `moved_sections[]` only and AC-BDY-38 holds; GIVEN a test that invokes BM4 BEFORE Layout's mutator (so `new_body_id` is not live in Layout, or a `moved_sections[]` member is not owned by it), THEN BM4 returns `-BDY_REBASE_UNKNOWN_BODY` (new body not live) or `-BDY_REBASE_SECTION_NOT_OWNED` (a member not owned by it), writes nothing, the sink records the code and the tables are consistent (the order is Assembly's contract, KRN:197; Assembly's `apply` then faults the kernel, AC-MSA-142) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **AC-BDY-41 (auto, integration) Weld then cut conservation [D.8, BDY-23].** GIVEN the absorbed body's socket section at `(120, 0)` in its own frame, `S = frame_shift_px = (576, 96)`, `world(b) - world(a) == S` and equal `vel` (docked), and an actor at `p = (220, 50)` on the absorbed body, WHEN the weld applies and then the cut of the same seam applies, THEN after the weld `pos_curr = (796, 146)`, after the cut `pos_curr = (100, 50)` (final minus original is `(-120, 0)`, because the cut re-bases the minimum-id moved section to `(0, 0)`), `abs` is conserved at both steps, and `actor_local_px` is `(100, 50)` before the weld, after the weld and after the cut.
- **AC-BDY-100 (auto, unit) Weld of a previously welded body [D.4].** GIVEN the absorbed body's socket section at `(120, 0)` in its own frame, placed at `(696, 96)` in the host (so `frame_shift_px = (576, 96)`, the DIFFERENCE and not the new offset), and an actor at `(220, 50)`, WHEN BM3 applies, THEN `pos_curr = (796, 146)` and `actor_local_px` is `(100, 50)` before and after.
- **AC-BDY-101 (auto, unit) Weld across different velocities [Edge Cases].** GIVEN host `vel (2, 0)` and absorbed `vel (0, 0)` with `docked` false, WHEN BM3 applies, THEN the host pose row is unchanged, the absorbed row is gone, every moved actor lands at `pos_curr + frame_shift_px`, no host actor moves, and `delta_abs` equals the D.8 value.
- **AC-BDY-79 (auto, unit) Weld with no actors on the absorbed body [BDY-22].** GIVEN an absorbed body with no actors, WHEN BM3 applies, THEN its pose row is deleted, `BODY_DOCK_CONTACT` is raised once, no `BODY_TRANSFER` is raised and the dirty set is exactly the one `BODY` key; GIVEN a host actor standing in the host's hatch passage, THEN it is untouched and has no event.
- **AC-BDY-82 (auto, unit) Same-tick weld and step [BDY-22].** GIVEN an actor on the absorbed body holding a key, WHEN BM3 applies in P2 and `body.integrate` runs in P3 of the same tick, THEN the actor is stepped exactly once from the shifted position, `pos_prev` equals the shifted pre-step `pos_curr`, and the interpolation delta that tick equals the step alone (no transfer artefact in the interpolated pose).
- **AC-BDY-42 (auto, integration) Hatch cap, player origin [BDY-24, D.11].** GIVEN an actor at `pos = (690, 282)` and the seam's two hatch rectangles, WHEN `actors_overlapping` is called, THEN it lists the actor (ascending) and Assembly's real `validate` of a `cause CHANNEL` cut rejects it (`MSA.CUT_BLOCKED`, MSA-21; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was a stub, Assembly is Designed)*); GIVEN `pos = (640, 250)`, THEN the list is empty and the cut proceeds.
- **AC-BDY-43 (auto, integration) Hatch cap, system origin [BDY-24, BDY-16].** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)* GIVEN a direct BM4 call with the actor at `pos = (690, 282)` inside `cap_rects` (the seam's two hatch plates `[672, 696)` and `[696, 720)` x `[240, 336)`), THEN it returns `-BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP`, the `actors` and `bodies` tables, the dirty set and the event queue are byte-identical to before (the check-then-write harness of AC-BDY-134 extended) and the sink recorded `BDY_PLAN_MISMATCH` 1. GIVEN a SYSTEM-origin (`cause CASCADE`) cut with the actor in the passage under Assembly's real deferral (MSA-22: defer while `actors_overlapping` is non-empty, 8.3; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was an Assembly stub)*), THEN Assembly defers the cut every tick the actor is in the passage and the cut applies on the first tick the list is empty (on expiry it is abandoned, never forced). The cap-trap actor's exposure on the restore path is AC-BDY-29b.
- **AC-BDY-44 (auto, integration with the real Layout and the Derelict Authoring `pod` template; the caller is Web Map & Transit's real `pod_hop` apply once its code exists, else its test double *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: Web Map & Transit and Derelict Authoring are Designed; was "provisional — Web Map & Transit undesigned")*) Pod hop [D.6, BDY-25].** GIVEN a pod body built by Layout from the Derelict Authoring pod template (DA-3, spawn `(72, 48)`) with one section at offset `(0, 0)`, and an actor on the home body at `(300, 100)` with `carry_ref` 17 (any non-zero opaque value), `last_octant` 1 and `sub = (106, -106)`, WHEN BM5 applies with `spawn_local (72, 48)`, THEN `pos_curr = (72, 48)`, `pos_prev = (72, 48)`, `sub = (0, 0)`, `last_octant = 1`, `carry_ref` is byte-identical in `controller.state`, and one `BODY_TRANSFER{pod}` is raised; GIVEN an unbuilt destination, THEN `can_transfer` answers `BDY_PLACE_UNKNOWN_BODY`; GIVEN an arrival cell inside a footprint, THEN `BDY_PLACE_INSIDE_FOOTPRINT` and no search for a free cell.
- **AC-BDY-102 (auto, unit) Transfer touches only its fields [Edge Cases].** GIVEN a seat that is DEAD and a seat with `carry_ref` set, WHEN each is transferred by BM3, BM4 and BM5, THEN the diff of the `actors` row shows only `body_id`, `pos_curr`, `pos_prev` and `sub` changed (`sub` only for BM4 and BM5), and the canonical bytes of `controller.state` are identical before and after.
- **AC-BDY-103 (auto, unit) Hop mid-step [Edge Cases].** GIVEN a seat holding octant 1 (NE) with `sub = (106, -106)` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was octant 0 with `sub.x = 100`, a state the sub invariant never produces)*, WHEN BM5 applies in P2 and a Controller stub writes `v_eff = 0` (`IN_POD`) in P3, THEN the P3 step leaves `pos_curr` at the arrival cell, `sub == (0, 0)` and `last_octant == 8`.
- **AC-BDY-128 (auto, unit + integration with Save/Load) A cut is always restorable [BDY-21, BDY-23, D.5, D.7].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* GIVEN Layout's `fixture_chain` and the old body at `world = (4,194,304, 0)` (exactly `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT`), `vel (3, 0)`, `frame_shift_px = (576, 96)`, WHEN `can_rebase_detach(old_body_id, (576, 96))` is read, THEN it answers `BDY_REBASE_POSE_OUT_OF_RANGE` (the new `world.x` would be 4,194,880); GIVEN the same body at `world = (4,193,728, 0)` (new `world.x` exactly 4,194,304), THEN it answers OK, BM4 applies, and `restore(snapshot())` accepts the block and is byte-identical (a cut at the limit is restorable); GIVEN `vel_old = (16, 0)` and a non-zero drift injected by a test build, THEN `BDY_VEL_OUT_OF_RANGE`; GIVEN a caller that ignores the read and applies BM4 at `world.x = 4,194,304`, THEN BM4 returns `OK` (the saturate class, BDY-21), the new pose row is saturated to `(4,194,304, 96)` with `vel (0, 0)`, `BDY_WORLD_LIMIT` and `BDY_PLAN_MISMATCH` each count once (the sink records the saturation), `restore(snapshot())` still accepts the block (no `BDY_RESTORE_POSE_RANGE`), and the actor-table bytes are identical to the run with a body at `world (0, 0)` (BDY-2); there is no build-dependent branch *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The same fixture with `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` lowered by a test configuration rejects an old save with `BDY_RESTORE_POSE_RANGE`, which is the evidence for the "lowering = schema bump" rule.
- **AC-BDY-45 (auto, unit) Orphans persist [BDY-4].** GIVEN a cut that leaves `C_new` with no actor, WHEN BM4 applies, THEN the new pose row exists, `actors_on(new_body_id)` is empty and no body is discarded by Body.
- **AC-BDY-81 (auto, unit; recording sink) Mutator order and retired bodies [BDY-21].** GIVEN one `WriteScope` applying Graph, Layout, Body in that order, WHEN a BM6 names a body that Layout retired earlier in the same scope, THEN `can_set_velocity` on the pre-state answers OK (the plan error is the caller's), and in `apply` BM6 returns `-BDY_VEL_UNKNOWN_BODY`, writes nothing and the sink records it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **AC-BDY-127 (auto, unit) BM7 pose setter: range and interior invariance [BDY-21, BDY-26].** GIVEN a live body with actors, WHEN `can_set_pose` is asked for a coordinate of exactly `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` and of `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` + 1 on either axis, THEN the first answers OK and the second `BDY_POSE_OUT_OF_RANGE`, and an unknown body answers `BDY_POSE_UNKNOWN_BODY`; WHEN BM7 sets a new pose, THEN `world_pose` returns the new `(world_x, world_y)` with `vel` unchanged, exactly `BODY/<body_id>` is marked dirty, no event is raised, and the `canonical_encode` of the payload's `actors` value is identical before and after. *(consistency pass 2026-10-27; Web Map ASK-B1, AC-WM-89 is the caller-side twin.)*
- **AC-BDY-112 (auto, unit) Rotation cannot be written [BDY-26].** GIVEN BM1 to BM7, THEN none accepts a heading argument and `heading_q` is 0 in every row after every mutator.
- **AC-BDY-132 (auto, unit; recording sink) Return-the-code class, one fixture per defect [BDY-21].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN each defect of class (i) in BDY-21 (BM2 and BM5 unknown actor; BM3 and BM4 equal ids; BM3, BM4, BM5 and BM6 and BM7 unknown or dead body; BM4 `moved_sections[]` member not owned by `new_body_id`; BM3 and BM4 `frame_shift_px` off the 24-px grid; BM4 an actor overlapping `cap_rects[]` (`-BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP`); BM1 over-cap, bad kind, duplicate seat and footprint cell; BM5 arrival inside a SOLID rectangle; BM6 velocity beyond `VEL_MAX_PX_PER_TICK`; BM7 coordinate beyond `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT`), WHEN the mutator is called, THEN it returns the matching negative `FAULT_CODE` (BM1 never returns an id), the sink records `(mutator_id, code)` once, and the canonical bytes, `next_actor_id`, the dirty set and the event queue equal their pre-call values; AND GIVEN a successful BM1, THEN it returns `actor_id` 1, 2, 3 in call order with no allocation per call (the return is an int).
- **AC-BDY-133 (auto, unit; recording sink) Saturate-and-continue class [BDY-21, BDY-23].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN a BM4 whose new pose row would leave `±WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` or whose `vel` would leave `±VEL_MAX_PX_PER_TICK` (the fixtures of AC-BDY-128), WHEN it applies, THEN it returns `OK`, the row is saturated as BDY-23 states, the sink records the saturation and `restore(snapshot())` accepts the block; GIVEN a BM4 that has BOTH an unknown `new_body_id` AND a range overflow, THEN class (i) wins: it returns `-BDY_REBASE_UNKNOWN_BODY` and writes nothing (no saturated row is written for a cut that did not happen).
- **AC-BDY-134 (auto, unit) Check-then-write: no half-applied weld or cut [BDY-21, P1].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN a BM3 with 8 actors on the absorbed body and `frame_shift_px` off the 24-px grid, and a BM4 with 8 actors on `C_new` and a `moved_sections[]` member not owned by `new_body_id`, and a BM4 with 8 actors on `C_new` one of which overlaps `cap_rects[]` (`-BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP`) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*, WHEN each is called, THEN each returns its `FAULT_CODE` and NONE of the 24 actor rows, no pose row, no dirty key and no event changed (a defect found for any row is found before the first row is written).
- **AC-BDY-135 (auto, integration; stub-backed until Assembly's story lands) A cut at the world limit is rejected at validate [BDY-21, D.5].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN Assembly's cut `validate` (the real one where its code exists, else a double that passes V3's `frame_shift_px` as the real one must) and an old body at `world.x = 4,194,304` with `frame_shift_px (576, 96)`, WHEN the cut is validated, THEN `can_rebase_detach(old_body_id, (576, 96))` answers `BDY_REBASE_POSE_OUT_OF_RANGE`, the cut is rejected and no mutator is called; GIVEN `world.x = 4,193,728` (the new `world.x` exactly the limit), THEN the cut validates.
- **AC-BDY-140 (auto, unit; recording sink) BM4 cap guard [BDY-24 (iii), BDY-21].** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)* GIVEN actors at `pos = (690, 282)` (box `[682, 698) x [282, 290)`; `ov` 112 and 16 with the two hatch plates `[672, 696)` and `[696, 720)` x `[240, 336)`) and at `(640, 250)`, WHEN BM4 is called with `cap_rects` = those plates, THEN it returns `-BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP`; with only the `(640, 250)` actor on the body it returns `OK` and applies normally.

### F. Pose, velocity and the inertial interior

- **AC-BDY-46 (auto, unit) Interior invariance [D.9, BDY-2].** GIVEN identical inputs on a 600-tick stream (mixed walk, run, stop, wall contact and a door closing by the real door hook) and `vel = (3, 0)` versus `(0, 0)` on the avatar's body, WHEN the stream runs, THEN the `canonical_encode` of the payload's `actors` value is identical at every tick (the whole-block `block_hash` differs by design and is NOT the criterion) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*.
- **AC-BDY-47 (lint) No interior read of the pose [BDY-2].** GIVEN the Body module, `solid_in`'s call path, the Emitter, Suit and the interior camera pose code, WHEN searched, THEN none names `world_` or `vel_` outside the allow-list *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the earlier form could not pass, because Body's own snapshot, restore and mutators must name the fields)*. Within the Body module the allow-list is the functions `integrate_pose` (the pose step of `body.integrate`), `snapshot`, `restore`, `post_restore`, BM3, BM4, BM6, BM7, `can_rebase_detach`, `can_set_velocity`, `can_set_pose`, `docked`, `world_pose`, `canvas_offset_render` and the projector script, kept in the committed file `tests/fixtures/body/pose_read_allowlist.txt`; a function not in it that names either token fails the lint. The other searched code (`solid_in`'s call path, the actor loop of `body.integrate`, the Emitter, Suit, the interior camera pose code) has an EMPTY allow-list, so any hit there fails.
- **AC-BDY-48 (auto, unit) Pose integration, canvas offset and freeze [D.7, BDY-9, BDY-26].** GIVEN body A at `world (0, 0)` (the avatar's body) and pod B at `world (600, 240)` with `vel (3, 0)`, WHEN 100 ticks run, THEN B is at `(900, 240)`, `canvas_offset(B) = (900, 240)` and `canvas_offset(A) = (0, 0)`; WHEN 3,600 ticks run from rest at `(0, 0)` with `vel (3, 0)`, THEN `world.x == 10,800`. GIVEN `world_x = 4,194,000` and `vel_x = 16`, THEN `world_x` reaches `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` (4,194,304) after 19 ticks, on tick 20 `vel` becomes `(0, 0)` with `world` unchanged and `BDY_WORLD_LIMIT` is 1, and after 100 further ticks it is STILL 1 (a single event, not repeated). GIVEN `can_set_velocity` with `(16, -16)` THEN OK; with `(17, 0)` and `(0, -17)` THEN `BDY_VEL_OUT_OF_RANGE`; with an unknown body THEN `BDY_VEL_UNKNOWN_BODY`.
- **AC-BDY-129 (auto, unit) Interpolated exterior offset and its resets [BDY-26, D.7].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* GIVEN avatar body A at `world (0, 0)` and pod B at `world (900, 240)` with `vel (3, 0)` on a clear run, WHEN `canvas_offset_render(B, tick_alpha)` is read at `tick_alpha` 0, 0.5 and 1 on tick `t`, THEN it is `floor((897, 240) + (3, 0) * alpha)` = `(897, 240)`, `(898, 240)`, `(900, 240)` (all integers, one floored offset per body); GIVEN a BM7 mid-leg re-origin applied in P2 of tick `t` that translates A and B by `V = (-600, 0)` in one apply, WHEN the next frame's `canvas_offset_render(B, tick_alpha)` is read for every `tick_alpha` in {0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1}, THEN it never differs by more than the relative speed `|vel_B - vel_A|` = 3 px from the previous frame's value and never by `|V|` = 600 (no smear), `world_prev` equals `world` for both rows on that tick, and the actor-table bytes are identical to the run without the re-origin; GIVEN a single-body BM7 (B from `(900, 240)` to `(912, 240)`, A untouched), THEN `canvas_offset_render(B, alpha)` is `(912, 240)` for every `alpha` on the next frame; GIVEN a BM5 that changes the avatar's body, THEN both `off_prev` and `off_curr` are computed against the new avatar body and the first post-switch frame has no interpolation across two frames of reference; GIVEN `post_restore`, THEN `world_prev == world` for every row and `snapshot()` is byte-identical with and without `world_prev` (it is never in the bytes).
- **AC-BDY-49 (auto, unit) Heading locked [BDY-26].** GIVEN a save with `heading_q != 0`, WHEN restored, THEN `BDY_RESTORE_HEADING_UNSUPPORTED` is reported and no state is applied.
- **AC-BDY-50 (auto, unit) Docking invariant and weld legality [D.8, BDY-27].** GIVEN `world(h) = (1000, 500)`, `world(b) = (1576, 596)`, `frame_shift_px (576, 96)` and equal `vel`, WHEN `docked(h, b, frame_shift_px)` is read, THEN true and `delta_abs == (0, 0)`; GIVEN `world(b) = (1600, 596)`, THEN false and `delta_abs == (-24, 0)` (D.8: `frame_shift_px - (world(b) - world(a))`) while the weld still succeeds; GIVEN the first fixture but `vel(b) = vel(a) + (1, 0)`, THEN `docked` is false even though `world(b) - world(a) == (576, 96)`. Weld legality reads no pose: `can_rebase_attach`, the Layout `can_attach` stub and the Graph `can_join` stub return identical answers over 1,000 pose pairs drawn from a seed list committed in the fixture (not at test time).
- **AC-BDY-51 (auto, integration) Interior inertial [BDY-28].** GIVEN a weld, a cut and a pod hop during which the avatar holds a key, WHEN the actor table is compared with the same run without any pose motion, THEN no actor's `pos_curr` differs except by the stated transfer arithmetic; there is no code path that writes `pos_curr` from `vel`.
- **AC-BDY-78 (auto, unit) Sparse pose rows and body count [3.3, BM6].** GIVEN a body with `world = (0, 0)` and a non-zero `vel`, WHEN BM6 sets `vel` to `(0, 0)`, THEN the pose row is deleted and the state is `STATIC`; GIVEN a body with no actor and no pose row, THEN `body_count()` does not count it and `actors_on(body_id)` is empty.
- **AC-BDY-76 (auto, unit) Event payloads [3.7, BDY-34].** GIVEN a weld, a cut and a pod hop, WHEN the P5 batch is read, THEN each `BODY_TRANSFER{weld | detach | pod}` carries `{tick, actor_id, seat, old_body, new_body, frame_delta_px}` (`frame_delta_px`, signed new minus old as defined in 3.7, equal to `+frame_shift_px`, `-frame_shift_px` and `to_pos - pos_curr(old)` respectively: `(576, 96)` for the `fixture_airlock` weld, `(-576, -96)` for the `fixture_chain` cut at the (1, 3) seam, and `(72, 48) - (300, 100) = (-228, -52)` for the pod hop of AC-BDY-44) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, `BODY_DOCK_CONTACT` carries `{tick, surviving_body, absorbed_body}`, `BODY_HULL_SEPARATION` carries `{tick, old_body, new_body}`, and no payload carries state a subscriber could use to desynchronise the simulation.

### G. Reads, sections and the read view

- **AC-BDY-52 (auto, integration) Section lookup [D.10, BDY-29].** GIVEN the airlock fixture (section 1 at `(0, 0)`, section 3 at `(576, 96)`), WHEN `actor_section` is read for an origin at `(695, 280)` and `(696, 280)`, THEN section 1 with local `(695, 280)` and section 3 with local `(120, 184)`; an origin in an exterior gap answers NONE; no tie rule is consulted (containment of half-open sets).

(This is the Body half of the noise-ordering question; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: Open Question 5 is ANSWERED, option (b), `controller.noise` is registered in the Controller's GDD (PCI L58, L139, L149), and AC-BDY-X1 is its live same-tick half, so this criterion is an integration criterion against the real hook order, not a stub)*.)
- **AC-BDY-54 (auto, unit) Read-view class [BDY-30].** GIVEN a PRESENTATION subscriber, WHEN it reads `pos_curr`, `pos_prev`, `actor_section` and the foot boxes, THEN the reads succeed; no `PERCEPTION_OUT` or per-player variant of any Body read exists.

### H. Persistence and restore

- **AC-BDY-55 (auto, unit) Round trip, three phases [BDY-31].** GIVEN a 600-tick scripted run in three phases (cardinal, diagonal with `sub != 0`, stopped), WHEN `snapshot(restore(snapshot()))` is compared at every tick, THEN it is byte-identical (equal `canonical_encode` output) for `body.state` alone and `post_restore` sets `pos_prev := pos_curr` without touching `sub`.
- **AC-BDY-15 (auto, unit) PC-18 tests 10 (re-fixtured) and 12, restore continuation [D.14, BDY-31].** GIVEN NE at `v_eff` 2 with `sub.x = 212` and `last_octant = 1` after tick 2, WHEN snapshot, restore with the same key held (the Adapter's resync lands in P2 of the first post-restore tick) and one tick run, THEN `pos_prev == pos_curr` after `post_restore`, `sub` is preserved, and tick 3 gives step 2 and `sub.x = 62`.
- **AC-BDY-77 (auto, unit) Late resync, the stated GIVEN [BDY-31, D.14].** GIVEN the AC-BDY-15 fixture but with the Adapter's resync landing one tick AFTER the first post-restore tick, WHEN that tick runs, THEN `move_octant` reads NONE against the persisted `last_octant`, `sub` resets (a loss of at most 255/256 px) and the cadence restarts; the test documents that this is not a correctness defect and that AC-BDY-15's continuity claim assumes the resync lands in P2 of the first post-restore tick.
- **AC-BDY-57 (auto, unit) Released key across the window [BDY-31].** GIVEN a snapshot mid-move and a restore with no key held, WHEN one tick runs, THEN `sub` resets exactly as a stop does.
- **AC-BDY-56 (auto, unit) Restore validation, one defect per fixture [BDY-31].** GIVEN a save carrying exactly one of the defects below, WHEN restored, THEN the named reason is reported, the WHOLE block is rejected, the live state is byte-identical to before the failed attempt, and a foot box overlapping a now-SOLID rectangle is NOT a failure. Each row is its own pass/fail.

  | Fixture | Expected reason |
  |---|---|
  | `schema_version` 2 | `BDY_RESTORE_VERSION` |
  | *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* `pos_x` stored as the float `100.0`; `pos_x` as `100.5`; `sub_x` as a string; `kind` as a bool; a missing `last_octant` (five rows; a float with an integral value is still a float) | `BDY_RESTORE_TYPE` (five rows; the whole block is rejected, nothing is truncated, the live state is byte-identical to before) |
  | duplicate `actor_id`; `actor_id` 0; `actor_id >= next_actor_id` | `BDY_RESTORE_ACTOR_ID` (three rows) |
  | `kind` 7; `seat` -1; `AGENT` with `seat` 2; `SEAT` with `seat` 0; two `SEAT` rows with one seat | `BDY_RESTORE_KIND` (five rows) |
  | `last_octant` 9; `last_octant` -1 | `BDY_RESTORE_OCTANT` |
  | `sub.x` 256 | `BDY_RESTORE_SUB_RANGE` |
  | `sub = (3, 0)` with `last_octant` 8; `sub.x` -3 with octant 0 (E); `sub.y` 4 with octant 0; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* `sub = (106, 0)` with octant 1 (NE); `sub = (106, 105)` with octant 1; `sub = (106, 106)` with octant 1 (the wrong sign on y) | `BDY_RESTORE_SUB_INVARIANT` (six rows) |
  | `odometer` -1 | `BDY_RESTORE_ODOMETER` |
  | `heading_q` 1 | `BDY_RESTORE_HEADING_UNSUPPORTED` |
  | `world_x` 2^22 + 1; `vel_x` 17 | `BDY_RESTORE_POSE_RANGE` (two rows) |
  | `MAX_ACTORS + 1` rows | `BDY_RESTORE_CAPACITY` |
  | *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* `n_actors` -1; `actors` column of 9 values with `n_actors` 1 | `BDY_RESTORE_COUNT` (two rows) |
  | body rows with `body_id` 2 then 1; two body rows both `body_id` 1; distinct actor rows with `actor_id` 5 then 3 | `BDY_RESTORE_ORDER` (three rows) |
  | a body row with `world (0, 0)` and `vel (0, 0)` | `BDY_RESTORE_NONCANONICAL` |
  | `body_id` not live in Layout (checked in `restore()` against Layout's already-restored state; the live state is unchanged, *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was `post_restore`)*) | `BDY_RESTORE_UNKNOWN_BODY` |
- **AC-BDY-58 (auto, unit) Unbound seat [BDY-7, 3.2].** GIVEN a `SEAT` row with no Controller row, WHEN a tick runs, THEN it is not moved, `sub == (0, 0)`, `last_octant == NONE` and `BDY_SEAT_UNBOUND` increments; an actor whose `body_id` is not live is skipped untouched with `BDY_ORPHAN_ACTOR`.
- **AC-BDY-59 (auto, integration) `REBASE_BLOCKS` exit [BDY-31].** GIVEN the Save/Load registry after Body's idempotence passes AC-BDY-55, WHEN `REBASE_BLOCKS` is read, THEN `body.state` is not listed (nor is `controller.state`: the registry value is `[interaction.state, assembly.state]`, Save/Load's) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*.
- **AC-BDY-137 (auto, unit) The s64 columns survive in memory [BDY-9, BDY-32].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN an `odometer` set to 2^53 + 7 and an `actor_id` of 2^40 through a test hook on the in-memory columns, WHEN they are read back before any snapshot and again after a snapshot and restore, THEN both equal the set values exactly (a `PackedInt32Array` column would fail this).
- **AC-BDY-110 (auto, unit) Schema v1 golden bytes [BDY-7, BDY-32].** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* GIVEN a fixed state (2 bodies, 3 actors, `odometer` 2^53 + 7), WHEN `canonical_encode(snapshot())` runs, THEN the bytes equal the committed golden file `tests/unit/body/golden/body_state_v1.bin`, frozen at the kernel spike by the independent reference encoder (kernel D.12), length 518 bytes: DICT header 1 + 8 = 9; keys 15 (`actors`) + 15 (`bodies`) + 17 (`n_actors`) + 17 (`n_bodies`) + 22 (`next_actor_id`) = 86; values `actors` 1 + 8 + 30 * 9 = 279, `bodies` 1 + 8 + 12 * 9 = 117, three INT 3 * 9 = 27; 9 + 86 + 279 + 117 + 27 = 518. A deliberate schema change must bump `schema_version` and replace the golden file.
- **AC-BDY-87 (auto, integration with a Save/Load stub) Death restart from autosave [BDY-31, P5].** GIVEN an autosave at tick N whose `body.state` canonical bytes are B0, WHEN the run proceeds to N + 600 with a full-crew death and the game restarts from that autosave, THEN `body.state` canonical bytes equal B0, `pos_prev == pos_curr` for every actor (asserted on Body's own `post_restore` derivation, BDY-31, not on any kernel or camera hook *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix)*), `next_actor_id` equals its autosave value, and no state of the lost 600 ticks is present in the block. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **This criterion runs against a Save/Load STUB**; the binding is covered by AC-BDY-138 (KRN-BIND-01) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*.
- **AC-BDY-138 (auto, integration through the real load path; INTEGRATION CLOSURE GATE of Body's epic, run in the composition-root story) A seat-1 command succeeds on the first post-restart tick [BDY-7, BDY-31, P5].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review.)* GIVEN the death restart of AC-BDY-87 with the real kernel and Save/Load registrations, WHEN the restore completes and the first `RUNNING` tick after it runs with a seat-1 move command queued, THEN the command passes the authority stage (no `ACTOR_NOT_OWNED`) and the seat's actor moves on that tick. The step is recorded (kernel CR-7, KRN-BIND-01; Save/Load SL-20, AC-SL-149) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6)*: the criterion runs against the real kernel and Save/Load registrations in the composition-root story, Body's epic does not close until it passes, and AC-BDY-87 does not cover the binding.
- **AC-BDY-119 (auto, integration) Restore order [BDY-5].** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the earlier text put Layout's `post_restore` before Body's `restore`, which contradicts the kernel's order.)* GIVEN the kernel restore sequence over the real registrations, THEN `layout.state`'s `restore()` precedes `body.state`'s `restore()`, `emitter.state`'s `restore()` (which lists `body.state` in `restore_after`) follows it, and EVERY block's `post_restore()` runs only after the LAST `restore()` of all blocks, in the same dependency order (kernel CR-8: `post_restore()` runs after all blocks restore).
- **AC-BDY-120 (auto, integration) A failed restore is a reported load failure [BDY-31].** GIVEN a save with an invalid `body.state` (any row of AC-BDY-56), WHEN the Save/Load stub loads it, THEN the load reports failure with the `BDY_RESTORE_*` reason, the live world is byte-identical to before the attempt and no exception is raised.

### I. Determinism, lint and diagnostics

- **AC-BDY-14 (auto, unit) PC-18 test 9, replay identity [BDY-32].** GIVEN the same command stream and the same Layout, WHEN it is run twice, THEN the `pos_curr` sequence is identical on every tick and the `body.state` canonical bytes are identical.
- **AC-BDY-109 (auto, unit) Order inside the hook [BDY-9, BDY-32].** GIVEN two moving bodies and three actors inserted as ids 3, 1, 2, WHEN one tick runs under a trace, THEN both pose updates precede the first actor step and the actors are stepped 1, 2, 3; GIVEN the same final state built by two different insertion histories, THEN `snapshot()` bytes are identical.
- **AC-BDY-63 (auto, unit) Tick-source independence [BDY-32].** GIVEN 1 to `MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME` ticks per frame, WHEN the same command stream runs, THEN `body.state` is identical; four catch-up ticks move an actor at most 12 px.
- **AC-BDY-106 (auto + lint) Zoom invariance [BDY-35].** GIVEN the same 600-tick stream run with the render stub reporting ladder step 1 and then `k_auto + 2`, THEN `body.state` bytes are identical; WHEN the Body module is searched, THEN `zoom`, `k_auto` and `field_h` appear nowhere outside the Presenter / projector directory and no Body function takes a zoom parameter.
- **AC-BDY-60 (lint) Forbidden tokens [BDY-3].** GIVEN the Body and Layout modules, WHEN searched, THEN there are zero matches *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: real tokens, with comment lines, string literals, the lint's own test directory and rejected-alternative prose excluded; the earlier list named concepts a grep cannot match)* for `RigidBody2D`, `CharacterBody2D`, `StaticBody2D`, `AnimatableBody2D`, `PhysicsBody2D`, `Area2D`, `PhysicsServer2D`, `move_and_slide`, `move_and_collide`, the call pattern `\btest_move\(`, `body_test_motion`, `physics_layer`, `NavigationServer2D`, `NavigationRegion2D` and `NavigationAgent2D`; zero matches in the block and hook for the patterns `\brandi\b`, `\brandf\b`, `\brandomize\b`, `\bTime\.`, `OS\.get_ticks`, `Engine\.get_physics_frames`, `: float\b`, `-> float\b`, `:= -?[0-9]+\.[0-9]`, `\bfloat\(` and `\bVector2\(` (the integer type is `Vector2i`, and `Vector2i(` is allowed); no script under `src/sim/body/` names `Node2D`, `Camera2D` or `Viewport` (the PROJECTOR lives in `src/presentation/projector/`, ADR-0010 section 1.2) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0010 sync)*; no tree node is a physics body (a scene check, not a grep). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* AND the GDScript typing warnings `UNTYPED_DECLARATION`, `INFERRED_DECLARATION`, `UNSAFE_*`, `NARROWING_CONVERSION`, `INTEGER_DIVISION` and `INFERENCE_ON_VARIANT` are set to Error for all of `src/` and `tools/`, which subsumes the Body and Layout directories (`debug/gdscript/warnings/directory_rules` exists on 4.7.2, ADR-0010 section 5; the CI gate is that every script loads) and the build reports zero warnings from files in them *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0010 sync)*; the only `/` between integers in `src/` is the annotated one inside `IntMath.trunc_div` (`src/shared/int_math.gd`), which a grep for `/` outside comments and strings confirms (`floor_div` calls it); and a **float-inference fixture** — a deliberately mis-typed scratch script under the lint's own test directory that assigns `a * 0.5` to an inferred `var` — makes the lint fail, proving the lint can see a silent float.
- **AC-BDY-61 (lint + auto) Canonical form [BDY-32].** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* GIVEN `snapshot()`, WHEN inspected, THEN it returns the five-key Dictionary of ints and two Arrays of ints (lint: no `Packed*Array`, float, `Vector2i`, `StringName` value or Dictionary-order iteration in the payload; no `encode_s32`, `encode_s64`, `var_to_bytes` (not used) or byte writer in Body source), it iterates ascending ids by sorting at snapshot time (never a `Dictionary` order), never places `odometer` in a `Vector2i`, and no derived or diagnostic value is in the payload (lint); the iteration-order half is proved by AC-BDY-109 (auto).
- **AC-BDY-62 (auto, unit) Diagnostics [BDY-34, 3.7].** GIVEN the defect fixtures of AC-BDY-26, AC-BDY-39, AC-BDY-48 and AC-BDY-58, WHEN run, THEN each counter increments exactly as stated, lives in the hook's diagnostics channel, and is absent from `snapshot()` and the hash; `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` reads 0 at the end of the clean stream: the MVP-slice home ship (5 compartments), one seat, a scripted 10,000-tick route (`tests/fixtures/body/clean_route_10k.*`) that walks every cardinal and diagonal octant, runs, stops, and opens and closes doors by the real door hook, with no system-origin cut and no restore. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: "the clean stream" is one scenario, id `clean_route_10k`, of the clean-build manifest `tests/fixtures/body/clean_build_manifest.yaml`; every other scenario id in it, listed in AC-BDY-29a, ends with `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP` and `BDY_TRAPPED_ACTOR` at 0 and every other defect counter at 0 except where the scenario is a deliberate-defect fixture and says so; the manifest, not prose, is the definition of "a clean build" for every criterion that uses the words.)*
- **AC-BDY-105 (auto, unit) Sweep-cost log [BDY-34].** GIVEN a test configuration with `SWEEP_COST_LOG_MS` = 0, WHEN an actor steps, THEN one log record per actor step carries the actor id and the microsecond cost; at the default threshold and a normal step no record is written; and (lint) `BDY_BLOCKED_TICKS` does not exist.
